=== \"granted\");\n    if (existing) {\n      license = existing;\n      pooledIntoExisting = true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!license) {\n    // Generate a unique license key with collision check.\n    let licenseKey = \"\";\n    const maxAttempts = 10;\n    for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt++) {\n      licenseKey = generateLicenseKey();\n      const collision = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n      if (!collision) break;\n      console.warn(`License key collision detected, retrying... (${attempt})`);\n      if (attempt >= maxAttempts) {\n        throw new Error(\"Failed to generate unique license key after max attempts\");\n      }\n    }\n\n    const user = await getOrCreateUser(customerEmail, {\n      name: customerName,\n      ...(stripeCustomerId ? { stripeCustomerId } : {}),\n    });\n    if (!user) {\n      throw new Error(`Failed to create user for ${customerEmail}`);\n    }\n\n    try {\n      license = await insertAccountKey({\n        key: licenseKey,\n        email: customerEmail.toLowerCase().trim(),\n        userId: user.id,\n        stripeCustomerId,\n        stripeSessionId: session.id,\n        status: \"granted\",\n      });\n    } catch (insertError: unknown) {\n      // Concurrent webhook delivery inserted the row first (unique\n      // stripe_session_id): fall back to the existing row.\n      if (\n        insertError &&\n        typeof insertError === \"object\" &&\n        \"code\" in insertError &&\n        (insertError as { code: string }).code === \"23505\"\n      ) {\n        console.log(\"License already inserted by concurrent request\");\n        license = await findAccountByStripeSession(session.id);\n      } else {\n        console.error(\"Failed to store license key:\", insertError);\n        throw insertError;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!license) {\n    throw new Error(`Failed to resolve minted license for session ${session.id}`);\n  }\n\n  // STEP 2: Grant the purchased credits (no included bundle on credit purchases).\n  // Idempotent on session.id, so safe to run on every delivery.\n  const grantResult = await grantCreditsForStripeEvent({\n    eventId,\n    eventType,\n    stripeObjectId: session.id,\n    userId: license.userId,\n    creditAmount,\n    sourceType: \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n    sourceId: session.id,\n  });\n\n  // Retry/duplicate delivery: credits already granted for this session — don't\n  // re-send the email. (Pooling resolves the same license by email each retry,\n  // so this guard is what keeps a re-delivery from emailing twice.)\n  if (grantResult === \"duplicate\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Credit purchase already processed for session ${session.id}, skipping email`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 3: Email. When we pooled into an existing key, send a top-up receipt\n  // with the new balance (the buyer already has this key). A genuinely new key\n  // gets the mint email with its starting balance.\n  if (pooledIntoExisting) {\n    console.log(\n      `Pooled ${creditAmount} credits into existing key ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... for ${customerEmail}`\n    );\n\n    const newBalance = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n    const emailResult = await emailService.sendCreditTopUp({\n      customerName,\n      customerEmail,\n      licenseKey: license.key,\n      creditAmount,\n      newBalance,\n      productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n      supportEmail: \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n    });\n\n    if (!emailResult.success) {\n      console.error(`Failed to send top-up email: ${emailResult.error}`);\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  console.log(\n    `Minted license ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... with ${creditAmount} credits for ${customerEmail}`\n  );\n\n  const emailResult = await emailService.sendCreditMint({\n    customerName,\n    customerEmail,\n    licenseKey: license.key,\n    creditAmount,\n    productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n    supportEmail: \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n  });\n\n  if (!emailResult.success) {\n    console.error(`Failed to send mint email: ${emailResult.error}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Process a charge refund.\n *\n * - \"license\" purchases: revoke the associated license.\n * - \"credits\" purchases: deduct the granted credits from the balance.\n *\n * REFUND SCOPE (per purchase type):\n * - license: acts only on a FULL refund (amount_refunded === amount).\n * - credits: the 6% processing fee is a separate, non-refundable line item, so a\n *   policy-compliant credit refund refunds only the credit value — a *partial*\n *   Stripe refund. Acts once amount_refunded covers the credit portion\n *   (charge total minus the non-refundable fee).\n *\n * TRACE PATH:\n * Charge -> PaymentIntent -> Checkout Session -> license_keys.stripe_session_id\n *\n * IDEMPOTENCY:\n * License revocation is idempotent (\"revoked\" status). Credit deduction is\n * recorded in stripe_processed_events keyed by charge.id, so retried\n * charge.refunded events never double-deduct.\n */\nexport async function handleChargeRefunded(\n  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n  eventId: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  console.log(\n    `Processing refund for charge ${charge.id} (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount})`\n  );\n\n  // STEP 2: Get PaymentIntent ID from the charge\n  const paymentIntentId =\n    typeof charge.payment_intent === \"string\"\n      ? charge.payment_intent\n      : charge.payment_intent?.id;\n\n  if (!paymentIntentId) {\n    console.log(\n      \"No payment_intent on charge, skipping (likely not a Checkout purchase)\"\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 3: Find the Checkout Session associated with this PaymentIntent\n  const sessions = await stripe.checkout.sessions.list({\n    payment_intent: paymentIntentId,\n    limit: 1,\n  });\n\n  if (sessions.data.length === 0) {\n    console.log(\n      `No checkout session found for payment_intent ${paymentIntentId}`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const checkoutSession = sessions.data[0];\n  const purchaseType = checkoutSession.metadata?.purchase_type;\n\n  // STEP 4: Route by purchase type, applying the right \"is this refund\n  // actionable?\" rule for each.\n  if (purchaseType === \"credits\") {\n    // The 6% fee is a separate, non-refundable line item, so a credit refund\n    // refunds only the credit value — a partial Stripe refund. Act once the\n    // refunded amount covers the credit portion (charge total minus the fee).\n    const feeCents = parseInt(checkoutSession.metadata?.fee_cents || \"0\", 10);\n    const creditPortion = charge.amount - feeCents;\n    if (charge.amount_refunded < creditPortion) {\n      console.log(\n        `Refund ${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount} does not cover the credit portion (${creditPortion}) for credits session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n      );\n      return;\n    }\n    await handleCreditRefund(charge, checkoutSession, eventId);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (purchaseType !== \"license\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Refund for unknown purchase type (${purchaseType}), skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // License purchases: act only on a FULL refund.\n  if (charge.amount_refunded !== charge.amount) {\n    console.log(\n      `Partial refund (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount}) for license session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Revoking license for checkout session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n\n  // STEP 5: Revoke the license in database\n  const license = await findAccountByStripeSession(checkoutSession.id);\n\n  if (!license) {\n    console.error(`License not found for session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  await refundCreditGrant({\n    sourceType: \"license_bundle\",\n    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n  });\n\n  // Already revoked - idempotent, just log and return\n  if (license.status === \"revoked\") {\n    console.log(`License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... already revoked`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 6: Update status to revoked\n  await updateAccountKey(license.id, { status: \"revoked\" });\n\n  console.log(\n    `License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... revoked due to full refund`\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Reverse a refunded credit-pack purchase.\n *\n * Looks up the original credit grant from the checkout session metadata and\n * atomically deducts it (clamped at 0). Idempotent via stripe_processed_events\n * keyed by charge.id, so webhook retries never double-deduct.\n */\nasync function handleCreditRefund(\n  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n  checkoutSession: Stripe.Checkout.Session,\n  eventId: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  // license_key is only present on top-ups; minted credit purchases carry none.\n  // The clawback is keyed on the checkout session id (the grant's source_id),\n  // so it works for both paths — license_key is informational only here.\n  const licenseKey = checkoutSession.metadata?.license_key;\n  const creditAmount = parseInt(\n    checkoutSession.metadata?.credit_amount || \"0\",\n    10\n  );\n\n  if (!creditAmount || creditAmount <= 0) {\n    console.error(\n      `Credit refund for session ${checkoutSession.id}: invalid metadata (credit_amount=${checkoutSession.metadata?.credit_amount}), skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const result = await refundCreditGrant({\n    sourceType: \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n  });\n\n  if (result.status === \"duplicate\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Credit refund already processed for charge ${charge.id}, skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const licenseHint = licenseKey ? ` (license ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...)` : \"\";\n  console.log(\n    `Deducted ${result.refundedAmount} credits for refunded charge ${charge.id}${licenseHint}`\n  );\n}\n","numLines":617,"startLine":1,"totalLines":617}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"79a532fc-7e41-4aeb-a07c-91c645a84719","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

checkout.session.completed\n *\n * SUPPORTED PURCHASE TYPES:\n * - \"license\": One-time license purchase\n * - \"credits\": Credit pack purchase (adds to Stripe Billing Meter)\n *\n * SECURITY:\n * - Verifies webhook signature using STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET\n * - Uses raw body for signature verification (required by Stripe)\n */\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const body = await req.text();\n  const signature = req.headers.get(\"stripe-signature\");\n\n  if (!signature) {\n    console.error(\"Stripe webhook: No signature header\");\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"No signature\" }, { status: 400 });\n  }\n\n  const webhookSecret = process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET;\n  if (!webhookSecret) {\n    console.error(\"Stripe webhook: STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET not configured\");\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Webhook secret not configured\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  let event: Stripe.Event;\n\n  // Verify webhook signature\n  try {\n    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(body, signature, webhookSecret);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\n      \"Stripe webhook signature verification failed:\",\n      err instanceof Error ? err.message : err\n    );\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Webhook signature verification failed\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Stripe webhook received: ${event.type}`);\n\n  // Handle checkout session lifecycle events\n  if (\n    event.type === \"checkout.session.completed\" ||\n    event.type === \"checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded\"\n  ) {\n    const session = event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session;\n    const purchaseType = session.metadata?.purchase_type;\n\n    if (session.payment_status !== \"paid\") {\n      console.log(\n        `Stripe webhook: ${event.type} for ${purchaseType} session ${session.id} is ${session.payment_status}, waiting for payment success`,\n      );\n      return NextResponse.json({ received: true });\n    }\n\n    // Route to appropriate handler based on purchase type\n    if (purchaseType === \"license\") {\n      try {\n        await handleLicensePurchase(session);\n      } catch (error) {\n        console.error(\n          \"Stripe webhook: Error processing license purchase:\",\n          error\n        );\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"Failed to process license purchase\" },\n          { status: 500 }\n        );\n      }\n    } else if (purchaseType === \"credits\") {\n      try {\n        await handleCreditPurchase(session, event.id, event.type);\n      } catch (error) {\n        console.error(\n          \"Stripe webhook: Error processing credit purchase:\",\n          error\n        );\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"Failed to process credit purchase\" },\n          { status: 500 }\n        );\n      }\n    } else {\n      console.log(\n        `Stripe webhook: Unknown purchase type (${purchaseType}), skipping`\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (event.type === \"checkout.session.async_payment_failed\") {\n    const session = event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session;\n    const purchaseType = session.metadata?.purchase_type;\n\n    if (purchaseType === \"credits\") {\n      console.log(\n        `Stripe webhook: async credit payment failed for session ${session.id}`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Handle charge.refunded for license revocation / credit reversal\n  if (event.type === \"charge.refunded\") {\n    const charge = event.data.object as Stripe.Charge;\n    try {\n      await handleChargeRefunded(charge, event.id);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"Stripe webhook: Error processing refund:\", error);\n {"parentUuid":"31c70b3b-4902-4a33-bf62-3d989ff92fe2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"af3289ea267d4d91f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JznbxafbUkJfewHzAntzLC","type":"tool_result","content":"Web search results for query: \"Stripe Managed Payments checkout session refunds API merchant of record how to refund\"\n\nLinks: [{\"title\":\"How Managed Payments works | Stripe Documentation\",\"url\":\"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/how-it-works\"},{\"title\":\"Your Merchant of Record Provider | Stripe Managed Payments\",\"url\":\"https://stripe.com/managed-payments\"},{\"title\":\"Introduces Managed Payments, Stripe's merchant of record solution | Stripe Documentation\",\"url\":\"https://docs.stripe.com/changelog/clover/2026-02-25/managed-payments\"},{\"title\":\"Managed Payments | Stripe Documentation\",\"url\":\"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments\"},{\"title\":\"Adds support for Managed Payments, Stripe's merchant of record solution | Stripe Documentation\",\"url\":\"https://docs.stripe.com/changelog/dahlia/2026-04-22/managed-payments?locale=en-GB\"},{\"title\":\"Build a Checkout integration with Managed Payments | Stripe Documentation\",\"url\":\"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/set-up\"},{\"title\":\"Update a Stripe Checkout integration to use Managed Payments | Stripe Documentation\",\"url\":\"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/update-checkout\"},{\"title\":\"What Is a Merchant of Record? | Stripe\",\"url\":\"https://stripe.com/resources/more/merchant-of-record\"},{\"title\":\"Understand the merchant of record in a Connect integration | Stripe Documentation\",\"url\":\"https://docs.stripe.com/connect/merchant-of-record\"},{\"title\":\"Enable merchants on your platform to accept payments directly | Stripe Documentation\",\"url\":\"https://docs.stripe.com/connect/saas/tasks/accept-payment\"}]\n\nBased on the search results, here's what I found about Stripe Managed Payments checkout session refunds and the merchant of record:\n\n## Managed Payments Overview\n\nManaged Payments is the Stripe merchant of record solution for payments, tax, fraud, disputes, and customer support. The customer sees Link as the merchant of record, and sees purchases as Sold through Link.\n\n## Refund Handling\n\nYou can still respond directly to customers, issue refunds, update subscriptions, and handle product-related issues yourself. Your customers can contact Link support to request refunds. Stripe can issue refunds within 60 days of the original transaction in certain cases.\n\nWhen refunds are processed, when you or Stripe refund a transaction, the refund includes any sales tax the customer paid. Although the customer is refunded the full amount, Stripe is required to retain and remit the original sales tax amount on refunded transactions in certain jurisdictions.\n\n## Creating Checkout Sessions with Managed Payments\n\nTo enable Managed Payments, you must set the managed_payments parameter when creating a Checkout Session. The search results provide code examples showing how to include `managed_payments: { enabled: true }` in your Checkout Session creation request.\n\nHowever, the search results don't contain specific API documentation for refunding Managed Payments checkout sessions. For detailed API reference information on refund endpoints, I'd recommend checking the official Stripe API documentation directly.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"b6323108-3440-4be4-917c-d6e8cdd09569","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:39:56.641Z","toolUseResult":{"query":"Stripe Managed Payments checkout session refunds API merchant of record how to refund","results":[{"tool_use_id":"srvtoolu_01Wnf7pDrEX62joAhE1yZz7D","content":[{"title":"How Managed Payments works | Stripe Documentation","url":"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/how-it-works"},{"title":"Your Merchant of Record Provider | Stripe Managed Payments","url":"https://stripe.com/managed-payments"},{"title":"Introduces Managed Payments, Stripe's merchant of record solution | Stripe Documentation","url":"https://docs.stripe.com/changelog/clover/2026-02-25/managed-payments"},{"title":"Managed Payments | Stripe Documentation","url":"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments"},{"title":"Adds support for Managed Payments, Stripe's merchant of record solution | Stripe Documentation","url":"https://docs.stripe.com/changelog/dahlia/2026-04-22/managed-payments?locale=en-GB"},{"title":"Build a Checkout integration with Managed Payments | Stripe Documentation","url":"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/set-up"},{"title":"Update a Stripe Checkout integration to use Managed Payments | Stripe Documentation","url":"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/update-checkout"},{"title":"What Is a Merchant of Record? | Stripe","url":"https://stripe.com/resources/more/merchant-of-record"},{"title":"Understand the merchant of record in a Connect integration | Stripe Documentation","url":"https://docs.stripe.com/connect/merchant-of-record"},{"title":"Enable merchants on your platform to accept payments directly | Stripe Documentation","url":"https://docs.stripe.com/connect/saas/tasks/accept-payment"}]},"Based on the search results, here's what I found about Stripe Managed Payments checkout session refunds and the merchant of record:\n\n## Managed Payments Overview\n\nManaged Payments is the Stripe merchant of record solution for payments, tax, fraud, disputes, and customer support. The customer sees Link as the merchant of record, and sees purchases as Sold through Link.\n\n## Refund Handling\n\nYou can still respond directly to customers, issue refunds, update subscriptions, and handle product-related issues yourself. Your customers can contact Link support to request refunds. Stripe can issue refunds within 60 days of the original transaction in certain cases.\n\nWhen refunds are processed, when you or Stripe refund a transaction, the refund includes any sales tax the customer paid. Although the customer is refunded the full amount, Stripe is required to retain and remit the original sales tax amount on refunded transactions in certain jurisdictions.\n\n## Creating Checkout Sessions with Managed Payments\n\nTo enable Managed Payments, you must set the managed_payments parameter when creating a Checkout Session. The search results provide code examples showing how to include `managed_payments: { enabled: true }` in your Checkout Session creation request.\n\nHowever, the search results don't contain specific API documentation for refunding Managed Payments checkout sessions. For detailed API reference information on refund endpoints, I'd recommend checking the official Stripe API documentation directly."],"durationSeconds":7.33831562499702,"searchCount":1},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"31c70b3b-4902-4a33-bf62-3d989ff92fe2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"9ca946d0-4d84-4586-84ef-b5ce81e7f5ac","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"af3289ea267d4d91f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018r9RauHYZZXCX8GxSnt8iS","type":"tool_result","content":"# Stripe Managed Payments Changelog Analysis\n\n## API Version\nThe `managed_payments` parameter is introduced in API version **2026-02-25.preview**.\n\n## Is it a Breaking Change?\nNo, it is **not a breaking change**. It only indicates that a `managed_payments` parameter can be set when creating a Checkout Session.\n\n## Key Quote from the Entry\n\n> \"新機能\n> \n> [Checkout Session](https://docs.stripe.com/api/checkout/sessions/create.md?api-version=2026-02-25.preview) の作成時に [managed_payments](https://docs.stripe.com/api/checkout/sessions/create.md?api-version=2026-02-25.preview#create_checkout_session-managed_payments) パラメーターを設定することで、Stripe のマーチャントオブレコードソリューションである [Managed Payments](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments.md) を有効にする機能を追加します。\"\n\n**Translation:** \"Add the ability to enable Managed Payments, Stripe's Merchant of Record solution, by setting the `managed_payments` parameter when creating a Checkout Session.\""}]},"uuid":"5637a9c5-1587-448c-88d7-4c0f2d6cfc10","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:40:13.015Z","toolUseResult":{"bytes":14727,"code":200,"codeText":"OK","result":"# Stripe Managed Payments Changelog Analysis\n\n## API Version\nThe `managed_payments` parameter is introduced in API version **2026-02-25.preview**.\n\n## Is it a Breaking Change?\nNo, it is **not a breaking change**. The parameter is marked as \"Added\" (not \"Removed\" or \"Modified\"), which means it's a new optional feature.\n\n## What Fields Are Added to Checkout Session?\nThe changelog indicates that the `managed_payments` parameter is added to:\n- `Checkout.Session` (object)\n- `Checkout.Session#create` (endpoint)\n\nHowever, the changelog does **not specify which individual fields** are added to the Checkout Session object itself. It only indicates that a `managed_payments` parameter can be set when creating a Checkout Session.\n\n## Key Quote from the Entry\n\n> \"新機能\n> \n> [Checkout Session](https://docs.stripe.com/api/checkout/sessions/create.md?api-version=2026-02-25.preview) の作成時に [managed_payments](https://docs.stripe.com/api/checkout/sessions/create.md?api-version=2026-02-25.preview#create_checkout_session-managed_payments) パラメーターを設定することで、Stripe のマーチャントオブレコードソリューションである [Managed Payments](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments.md) を有効にする機能を追加します。\"\n\n**Translation:** \"Add the ability to enable Managed Payments, Stripe's Merchant of Record solution, by setting the `managed_payments` parameter when creating a Checkout Session.\"","durationMs":5544,"url":"https://docs.stripe.com/changelog/clover/2026-02-25/managed-payments"},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9ca946d0-4d84-4586-84ef-b5ce81e7f5ac","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"c8806560-42d8-476e-a830-537b1c718cea","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"af3289ea267d4d91f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Hg3K9ML5Wmv8NfebQDqfRP","type":"tool_result","content":"# Managed Payments: PaymentIntent and Charge Ownership\n\nBased on the provided documentation, here's what can be determined:\n\n## Ownership of PaymentIntent and Charge Objects\n\nThe documentation states that when customers request data deletion:\n\n> Stripe deletes customer data from data objects used by or generated from Managed Payments transactions (including objects in your Stripe account). This includes **[PaymentIntent](https://docs.stripe.com/api/payment_intents.md)** and **[Charge](https://docs.stripe.com/api/charges/object.md)** objects.\n\nThis indicates that **PaymentIntent and Charge objects exist in the merchant's Stripe account**, but the specifics of ownership structure and whether `payment_intent` is returned on the checkout session are **not explicitly detailed** in this document.\n\n## Key Limitation\n\nThe documentation does **not address**:\n- Whether `payment_intent` appears on the checkout session response\n- The specific behavior of `charge.refunded` events on the merchant account\n- Whether `payment_intent` is null or unavailable in API responses\n- How refunds are issued via the API (beyond stating that merchants can execute refunds via dashboard or API)\n\n## What IS Documented\n\nThe document confirms:\n- Merchants retain full visibility and control to execute refunds and subscription updates via **dashboard or API**\n- Refund amounts include collected sales tax\n- Stripe handles chargeback responses on behalf of the merchant\n\n**For detailed API object behavior with Managed Payments, you would need to consult the Stripe API reference documentation or contact Stripe support directly.**"}]},"uuid":"3a112762-8b0a-418e-a6e4-a7bc50a0a4d2","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:40:33.071Z","toolUseResult":{"bytes":18601,"code":200,"codeText":"OK","result":"# Managed Payments: PaymentIntent and Charge Ownership\n\nBased on the provided documentation, here's what can be determined:\n\n## Ownership of PaymentIntent and Charge Objects\n\nThe documentation states that when customers request data deletion:\n\n> Stripe deletes customer data from data objects used by or generated from Managed Payments transactions (including objects in your Stripe account). This includes **[PaymentIntent](https://docs.stripe.com/api/payment_intents.md)** and **[Charge](https://docs.stripe.com/api/charges/object.md)** objects.\n\nThis indicates that **PaymentIntent and Charge objects exist in the merchant's Stripe account**, but the specifics of ownership structure and whether `payment_intent` is returned on the checkout session are **not explicitly detailed** in this document.\n\n## Key Limitation\n\nThe documentation does **not address**:\n- Whether `payment_intent` appears on the checkout session response\n- The specific behavior of `charge.refunded` events on the merchant account\n- Whether `payment_intent` is null or unavailable in API responses\n- How refunds are issued via the API (beyond stating that merchants can execute refunds via dashboard or API)\n\n## What IS Documented\n\nThe document confirms:\n- Merchants retain full visibility and control to execute refunds and subscription updates via **dashboard or API**\n- Refund amounts include collected sales tax\n- Stripe handles chargeback responses on behalf of the merchant\n\n**For detailed API object behavior with Managed Payments, you would need to consult the Stripe API reference documentation or contact Stripe support directly.**","durationMs":5691,"url":"https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/how-it-works"},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c8806560-42d8-476e-a830-537b1c718cea","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
{"parentUuid":"e14e14b6-7d0a-44c6-a917-a4d0b6be3995","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"af3289ea267d4d91f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018q9TNZ4zcETt5gg15xnfaY","type":"tool_result","content":"# Managed Payments and Checkout Sessions: Key Changes\n\nBased on the documentation provided, here's what changes when you enable Managed Payments:\n\n## Payment Processing Architecture\n\nThe documentation **does not explicitly state** whether a `payment_intent` still exists when `managed_payments[enabled]=true` is set on a Checkout Session. However, it indicates that:\n\n- **Stripe controls the Checkout Session**: As a merchant-of-record, Stripe takes control of certain aspects of the Checkout Session\n- **Tax and compliance handling**: Managed Payments handles tax calculation, withholding, fraud prevention, and chargeback management\n- **Limited to digital products**: Managed Payments only supports digital products (no shipping)\n\n## Unsupported Parameters\n\nWhen creating a Checkout Session with `managed_payments[enabled]=true`, you **must remove** these parameters:\n\n### Both Subscription and One-Time Payment:\n- `automatic_tax` / `tax_id_collection` (Managed Payments handles all tax)\n- `payment_method_configuration` / `payment_method_types` / `payment_method_options` (Dynamic payment methods used instead)\n- `customer_update[name]` / `customer_update[address]` (Auto-updated by Managed Payments)\n- `shipping_address_collection` / `shipping_options` (Not supported)\n\n### Subscription-specific:\n- `adaptive_pricing`, `subscription_data.default_tax_rates`\n- `subscription_data.application_fee_percent` / `on_behalf_of` / `transfer_data` (Connect not supported)\n- `subscription_data.invoice_settings` / `invoice_creation` (Managed Payments handles post-sale actions)\n\n### One-time Payment-specific:\n- `payment_intent_data.setup_future_usage`\n- `payment_intent_data.application_fee_amount` / `transfer_data` / `transfer_group`\n- `payment_intent_data.statement_descriptor*` / `receipt_email` / `shipping`\n\n## Refunds and Merchant Account Control\n\n**The documentation does not clarify refund handling or whether PaymentIntents/Charges are created on the merchant's own account.** This represents a significant gap in the provided content regarding the operational mechanics of Managed Payments at the API level."}]},"uuid":"774056e7-79af-461f-8753-92056b0c7c4b","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:40:36.113Z","toolUseResult":{"bytes":16646,"code":200,"codeText":"OK","result":"# Managed Payments and Checkout version 2025-12-15.clover release:\n\n## Key Features in the 2025-12-15.clover Release\n\nThe December release of Clover introduces Accounts v2 for Connect, allowing Connect platforms and marketplaces to manage connected accounts and customers, and map all user activity to a unified Account object.\n\nGlobal Payouts now support 12 new countries for cross-border payouts, and you can specify a payment purpose when creating an OutboundPayment to help ensure compliance with upcoming NACHA requirements.\n\nYou can now access additional bank account identification details when retrieving Payout Methods.\n\nAdditional specific enhancements documented for this version include:\n- The Accounts v2 API creates a single identity to represent your user across Stripe\n- Support for updating line items on existing Checkout Sessions with a custom UI\n- The ability to retrieve information about reserves\n- Additions of card payment method details to Payment Records\n- Subscriptions updated from the Customer Portal can now also modify their billing cycle anchor\n\nYou can find the complete changelog at the Stripe documentation on their changelog page for Clover releases.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown {"parentUuid":"7d6d9815-1cf4-4389-bde9-e625950f7bc8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"af3289ea267d4d91f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019SCZo5raj6eKfqNz44AdhH","type":"tool_result","content":"# Checkout Session Object Fields\n\nYes, the Checkout Session object includes all the fields you asked about. Here are the details:\n\n## `payment_intent`\n- **Type**: string\n- **Nullability**: nullable\n- **Expandable**: yes\n- **Description**: The ID of the PaymentIntent for Checkout Sessions in `payment` mode. You can't confirm or cancel the PaymentIntent for a Checkout Session. To cancel, [expire the Checkout Session](https://docs.stripe.com/docs/api/checkout/sessions/expire.md) instead.\n\n## `customer_details.email`\n- **Type**: string\n- **Nullability**: nullable\n- **Description**: The email associated with the Customer, if one exists, on the Checkout Session after a completed Checkout Session or at time of session expiry. Otherwise, if the customer has consented to promotional content, this value is the most recent valid email provided by the customer on the Checkout form.\n\n## `payment_status`\n- **Type**: enum\n- **Nullability**: not nullable (always present)\n- **Example value**: `\"unpaid\"` (from the sample object)\n- **Description**: Indicates the payment status of the session\n\n## `metadata`\n- **Type**: object\n- **Nullability**: nullable\n- **Description**: Set of [key-value pairs](https://docs.stripe.com/docs/api/metadata.md) that you can attach to an object. This can be useful for storing additional information about the object in a structured format.\n\n## `url`\n- **Type**: string\n- **Nullability**: not nullable\n- **Example**: `\"https://checkout.stripe.com/c/pay/cs_test_...\"`\n- **Description**: The URL where the Checkout Session can be accessed"}]},"uuid":"b2239d15-07fe-430d-9073-24595c38f9e2","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:41:42.184Z","toolUseResult":{"bytes":162634,"code":200,"codeText":"OK","result":"# Checkout Session Object Fields\n\nYes, the Checkout Session object includes all the fields you asked about. Here are the details:\n\n## `payment_intent`\n- **Type**: string\n- **Nullability**: nullable\n- **Expandable**: yes\n- **Description**: The ID of the PaymentIntent for Checkout Sessions in `payment` mode. You can't confirm or cancel the PaymentIntent for a Checkout Session. To cancel, [expire the Checkout Session](https://docs.stripe.com/docs/api/checkout/sessions/expire.md) instead.\n\n## `customer_details.email`\n- **Type**: string\n- **Nullability**: nullable\n- **Description**: The email associated with the Customer, if one exists, on the Checkout Session after a completed Checkout Session or at time of session expiry. Otherwise, if the customer has consented to promotional content, this value is the most recent valid email provided by the customer on the Checkout form.\n\n## `payment_status`\n- **Type**: enum\n- **Nullability**: not nullable (always present)\n- **Example value**: `\"unpaid\"` (from the sample object)\n- **Description**: Indicates the payment status of the session\n\n## `metadata`\n- **Type**: object\n- **Nullability**: nullable\n- **Description**: Set of [key-value pairs](https://docs.stripe.com/docs/api/metadata.md) that you can attach to an object. Stripe customer count (paginated, up to 100)\n26\t   * 2. Polar customer count (paginated)\n27\t   *\n28\t   * @returns { totalCustomers, totalCreditsUsed, polarCustomers, stripeCustomers }\n29\t   */\n30\t  get: adminProcedure.query(async () => {\n31\t    try {\n32\t      // Fetch Stripe customers count\n33\t      let stripeCustomers = 0;\n34\t      try {\n35\t        const allCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({ limit: 100 });\n36\t        stripeCustomers = allCustomers.data.length;\n37\t      } catch {\n38\t        // Stripe not configured\n39\t      }\n40\t\n41\t      // Fetch Polar customers count\n42\t      let polarCustomers = 0;\n43\t      try {\n44\t        const polarResult = await polarClient.customers.list({\n45\t          organizationId: POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID,\n46\t          limit: 100,\n47\t        });\n48\t\n49\t        for await (const page of polarResult) {\n50\t          if (page && \"items\" in page) {\n51\t            polarCustomers += (page as { items: unknown[] }).items.length;\n52\t          } else if (page && \"id\" in page) {\n53\t            polarCustomers++;\n54\t          }\n55\t        }\n56\t      } catch {\n57\t        // Polar not configured\n58\t      }\n59\t\n60\t      // Calculate total credits used\n61\t      // Note: Stripe's listEventSummaries requires a customer parameter,\n62\t      // so we'd need to sum across all customers. For now, return 0.\n63\t      const totalCreditsUsed = 0;\n64\t\n65\t      return {\n66\t        totalCustomers: stripeCustomers + polarCustomers,\n67\t        totalCreditsUsed,\n68\t        polarCustomers,\n69\t        stripeCustomers,\n70\t      };\n71\t    } catch (error) {\n72\t      console.error(\"Stats fetch error:\", error);\n73\t      throw new TRPCError({\n74\t        code: \"INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR\",\n75\t        message:\n76\t          error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Failed to fetch stats\",\n77\t      });\n78\t    }\n79\t  }),"}]},"uuid":"c32e5775-8a3a-4400-b950-d097f87197d8","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:42:09.464Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/server/api/routers/admin/stats.ts","content":"export const statsRouter = createTRPCRouter({\n  /**\n   * Get dashboard statistics.\n   *\n   * FETCHES:\n   * 1. Stripe customer count (paginated, up to 100)\n   * 2. Polar customer count (paginated)\n   *\n   * @returns { totalCustomers, totalCreditsUsed, polarCustomers, stripeCustomers }\n   */\n  get: adminProcedure.query(async () => {\n    try {\n      // Fetch Stripe customers count\n      let stripeCustomers = 0;\n      try {\n        const allCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({ limit: 100 });\n        stripeCustomers = allCustomers.data.length;\n      } catch {\n        // Stripe not configured\n      }\n\n      // Fetch Polar customers count\n      let polarCustomers = 0;\n      try {\n        const polarResult = await polarClient.customers.list({\n          organizationId: POLAR_ORGANIZATION_ID,\n          limit: 100,\n        });\n\n        for await (const page of polarResult) {\n          if (page && \"items\" in page) {\n            polarCustomers += (page as { items: unknown[] }).items.length;\n          } else if (page && \"id\" in page) {\n            polarCustomers++;\n          }\n        }\n      } catch {\n        // Polar not configured\n      }\n\n      // Calculate total credits used\n      // Note: Stripe's listEventSummaries requires a customer parameter,\n      // so we'd need to sum across all customers. For now, return 0.\n      const totalCreditsUsed = 0;\n\n      return {\n        totalCustomers: stripeCustomers + polarCustomers,\n        totalCreditsUsed,\n        polarCustomers,\n        stripeCustomers,\n      };\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"Stats fetch error:\", error);\n      throw new TRPCError({\n        code: \"INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR\",\n        message:\n          error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Failed to fetch stats\",\n      });\n    }\n  }),","numLines":60,"startLine":20,"totalLines":81}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5a3efc0b-af69-433b-86af-dfb76e049005","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

or mode=payment\n--data-urlencode \"success_url=https://localhost:4242/success\"\n```\n\nSpecific parameters like `allow_promotion_codes` or `customer_creation` are **not mentioned** in this documentation.\n\n---\n\n## Recommendation\n\nFor complete answers on these advanced topics, refer to:\n- [Checkout Session API Reference](https://docs.stripe.com/api/checkout/sessions/create.md)\n- [Managed Payments update guide](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/update-checkout.md)"}]},"uuid":"71941110-095d-41bc-8321-1cf5db4301df","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:43:14.023Z","toolUseResult":{"bytes":24058,"code":200,"codeText":"OK","result":"# Managed Payments Checkout Session Questions\n\nBased on the provided documentation, here's what I can confirm:\n\n## API Version Required\nThe documentation explicitly states:\n> `2025-03-31.basil` [以降](https://docs.stripe.com/changelog.md)の API バージョンを使用してください。\n\n**API version `2025-03-31.basil` or later is required.**\n\n---\n\n## Payment Intent Field & Refunds\n\nThe provided documentation **does not explicitly address**:\n- Whether `payment_intent` field is populated on Checkout Sessions with `managed_payments[enabled]=true`\n- Whether refunds can be created via `stripe.refunds.create` using a payment_intent from a Managed Payments session\n\nThis information is not included in the current page content.\n\n---\n\n## Unsupported Checkout Session Parameters\n\nThe documentation **does not provide a comprehensive list** of unsupported or restricted parameters for Managed Payments Checkout Sessions.\n\nThe only parameters explicitly shown as **required** are:\n```curl\n-d \"line_items[0][price]={{PRICE_ID}}\"\n-d \"line_items[0][quantity]=1\"\n-d \"managed_payments[enabled]=true\"\n-d mode=subscription  # {"parentUuid":"04daa09d-f40a-41a8-8a26-0c682ef7c6b2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af3289ea267d4d91f","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWHn9rTkbikDqT3T2CDu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## What I established\n\n**Q2 — Is the `as any` a real mismatch or narrowing noise? Narrowing noise, and the oracle's signal 5 is wrong.**\n\n`/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/node_modules/stripe/types/lib.d.ts:50` declares `apiVersion?: LatestApiVersion;`, and `LatestApiVersion = typeof ApiVersion` where `types/apiVersion.d.ts` is `export const ApiVersion = '2026-02-25.clover';`. That is a **single string literal type, not a union of supported versions**. So *every* pin other than the exact latest needs a cast — including the shared client's own `2025-12-15.clover`, which is why it also needed suppression. The `as any` therefore proves nothing about `2025-02-24.acacia` being retired. Both suppressions are compile-time artifacts of the SDK's documented design (\"This library's types only reflect the latest API version\").\n\n**Both suppressions are correctly scoped and self-policing.** `tsc --noEmit` on the repo passes with zero errors, which proves neither directive is unused (TS2578). I probed the exact error in a scratchpad harness: the `create()` call raises `TS2769: No overload matches this call … 'managed_payments' does not exist in type 'SessionCreateParams'`, reported **at the call line**, so the line-146 directive catches precisely it. `managed_payments` appears in **0 files** in the entire `stripe@20.4.1` package — correct, because Stripe shipped it on the `2026-02-25.**preview**` track, not GA. I also confirmed via a second probe that an *additional* error inside that literal shifts the diagnostic and surfaces as TS2578 rather than being swallowed, and that `session` keeps its real `Stripe.Checkout.Session` type (not `any`). And `cjs/stripe.core.js:76` → `cjs/RequestSender.js:229` passes `apiVersion` **verbatim** into the `Stripe-Version` header, so the `; managed_payments_preview=v1` suffix transmits correctly.\n\n**Q1 — What the admin router reads: exactly one Stripe field.** `session.payment_intent` (customers.ts:311,316-318); `refunds.create` reads nothing back. Stripe's own acacia-pinned reference documents `payment_intent` as `string, nullable, expandable` — identical to the installed SDK's `Checkout/Sessions.d.ts:220` (`payment_intent: string | Stripe.PaymentIntent | null`). **No shape divergence on the field actually read.** Same for every shared-client read (`customers.list().data[].id`, `session.url`, `customer_details.email`, `payment_status`, `metadata`, `charge.amount/amount_refunded/payment_intent`) — all version-stable core fields.\n\n**Q4 — Preview not enabled is fail-closed.** The route reads only `session.url` (a core field), and the whole handler is wrapped in try/catch returning HTTP 500 (`route.ts:212-222`), with an explicit `if (session.url)` / `else throw` guard at 205-211. A rejected preview header produces a **failed checkout**, never a silently wrong price or credit grant.\n\n**Q3 — Yes, and this is where the real defect is.** The clients *do* cross on the same objects, and Stripe publishes a hard version floor that the admin client sits below.\n\nBoth `docs.stripe.com/payments/managed-payments/set-up` and `.../update-checkout` state the same requirement verbatim: *\"`2025-03-31.basil` 以降の API バージョンを使用してください\"* — **use API version `2025-03-31.basil` or later.** The admin client is pinned to `2025-02-24.acacia`, five weeks **below** that floor, and it is the only client that ever retrieves and refunds those sessions.\n\nEvery session it touches is a Managed Payments session. `credits/route.ts:195` sets `managed_payments: { enabled: true }` on **all** checkout sessions, and that route's own docblock says it is \"the only way to obtain a key (the standalone license product is retired)\". The only writes to `license_keys.stripe_session_id` are `stripe-webhook.ts:103` (retired license path) and `:362` (`handleCreditMint`, the Managed Payments path) — and `refund` hard-requires that column at customers.ts:305.\n\nNote the admin `refund` mutation has **no try/catch** (customers.ts:291-342, unlike `list` and `updateEmail`), so a Stripe error propagates raw to the admin modal.\n\nOne adjacent, out-of-oracle observation worth passing on: that same handler claws back `sourceType: \"license_bundle\"` (customers.ts:332) while credit mints grant `sourceType: \"stripe_credit_pack\"` (stripe-webhook.ts:395) — the admin-side clawback is a silent no-op for every minted license.\n\n---\n\nCANDIDATE finding:\nfile: /Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/server/api/routers/admin/customers.ts\nline: 33\nsummary: The admin-only Stripe client is pinned to `2025-02-24.acacia`, which is below Stripe's documented `2025-03-31.basil` minimum for Managed Payments, yet it is the sole client used to retrieve and refund checkout sessions that are *always* created with `managed_payments: { enabled: true }` by a different client pinned 10 months later — and the `as any` on that line erased the only compile-time signal that this pin is unmodelled.\nfailure_scenario: A customer buys credits; `credits/route.ts:147` creates the session under `2025-12-15.clover; managed_payments_preview=v1` with Managed Payments on, and `stripe-webhook.ts:362` persists its id to `license_keys.stripe_session_id`. An admin later clicks \"Refund & Revoke\". `customers.ts:310` retrieves that Managed Payments session under sub-floor `2025-02-24.acacia` — a version that predates the feature entirely. Either Stripe rejects/downshifts the object and `session.payment_intent` comes back null, tripping the `BAD_REQUEST \"No payment intent found for this session\"` throw at 312, or the call errors and propagates raw through the un-try/caught mutation. Either way `refunds.create` at 323 never runs: the admin sees a failure in the refund modal, the customer's money is never returned, and because `updateAccountKey(..., \"revoked\")` at 338 sits after the refund, the license is not revoked either — the customer silently keeps working credits they were supposed to be refunded off, and the admin has no working refund path for any purchase the product actually sells.\ntrigger_path: Admin dashboard → `app/[locale]/user/(authenticated)/customers/CustomersClient.tsx:567` `refundMutation.mutate({ licenseKeyId, revokeLicense: true })` → tRPC `admin.customers.refund` → `server/api/routers/admin/customers.ts:310` `stripe.checkout.sessions.retrieve()` on the acacia-pinned client constructed at line 33 → line 311/323\noracle: [A4/A5]"}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3408,"cache_read_input_tokens":91630,"output_tokens":3949,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3408},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3949,"cache_read_input_tokens":91630,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3408,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3408,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdWHn8tAufioyLB5gQBbk","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","attributionMcpServer":"claude.ai Exa","attributionMcpTool":"web_search_exa","type":"assistant","uuid":"d3df0938-d3eb-44c4-8c89-8852381a4fc9","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:44:39.041Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Every pin except the exact latest needs a cast, which is why the shared client's own `2025-12-15.clover` also needed suppression. The `as any` says nothing about acacia being retired.\n2. **Both suppressions are necessary and correctly scoped.** `tsc --noEmit` passes clean, proving neither directive is unused (TS2578). Preview-not-enabled is fail-closed (reads only `session.url`, guarded, inside a try/catch returning 500).\n\nWhat *is* real: two Stripe doc pages state a hard floor — **API version `2025-03-31.basil` or later** for Managed Payments. An admin later clicks \"Refund & Revoke\". `customers.ts:310` retrieves that Managed Payments session under sub-floor `2025-02-24.acacia` — a version that predates the feature entirely. Either Stripe rejects/downshifts the object and `session.payment_intent` comes back null, tripping the `BAD_REQUEST \"No payment intent found for this session\"` throw at 312, or the call errors and propagates raw through the un-try/caught mutation.
